Let's design final algorithm:
- Maintain best sequence with exact score.
- For length n0=384 (and possibly other lengths), run a pipeline:
  A. Initialize f (normalized, positive). Options: parent, uniform, cosine tapers, maybe parent with random perturbations, and maybe length-resampled.
  B. Run smooth projected gradient to reduce M quickly, using top-K active logsumexp. This can move beyond coordinate local? Or coordinate after.
  C. Run coordinate descent with exact convex 1D multiple sweeps.
  D. Run LP direction line search (as previous) maybe to escape.
  E. Repeat with perturbed seeds.
- Use time management: allocate maybe 700s on length 384, 300s on length 512/768 exploratory, 100s final polish.

Coordinate descent algorithm details:
```
def coord_step(f, c, stop_time, max_sweeps, order_seed):
    n=len(f); L=2*n-1
    f = normalized copy; c=conv(f)
    M = max(c); best_score = 2n*M
    changed_sweeps=0
    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
       if time>stop: break
       improved=False
       order = rng.permutation(n) or maybe optimize coordinates sorted by potential? We can update in random order.
       for i in order:
          if time>stop - small: break
          v=f[i]; R=1-v
          if R <= 1e-14:
              # if near one-hot, reset to uniform? maybe perturb
              continue
          start=i; end=min(i+n, L); diag=2*i
          E = np.zeros(L); E[start:end] = 2*f[:end-start]; E[diag] -= 2*v
          N = c - v*E
          N[diag] -= v*v
          # ensure N >=0? clip small negative
          # But computing E,N each coord O(L) allocation. Could optimize E as sparse? OK.
          H = lambda y: max(alpha*N + beta*E + y*y at diag)
          # golden on [0,1]. But if old v, H(v)=M. We can use interval [0,1].
          yopt, Hmin = minimize_convex(H, 0,1, iterations=35)
          if Hmin < M - 1e-14:
              alpha_ref, beta_ref = ...
              c_new = alpha_ref*N + beta_ref*E
              c_new[diag] += yopt*yopt
              c_new = np.maximum(c_new,0)
              f[i]=yopt
              f[np.arange(n)!=i] *= (1-yopt)/R  # but excluding i, if i? Use mask or scale all then set i?
              # scaling all: f *= (1-yopt)/R; f[i]=yopt. But f[i] old scaled then set.
              f *= (1-yopt)/R
              f[i] = yopt
              c = c_new
              M = float(max(c))
              improved=True
       if not improved: break
    # after each sweep normalize f and recompute c to correct drift
    f = normalized; c=conv; M=max
    return f,M
```

def anchor_f(u):
    u = np.asarray(u)
    m = np.max(u)
    e = np.exp(u-m)
    denom = e.sum()+1.0
    f = np.empty(n); f[:-1]=e/denom; f[-1]=1/denom
    return f
```
Objective:
```
def obj_grad_u(u, tau):
    f=anchor_f(u)
    c=conv(f)
    lse = (1/tau)*logsumexp(c/tau)
    # weights p
    z = c/tau; z -= z.max(); p=exp(z); p/=p.sum()
    Hf = correlate(p, f, 'valid')  # length n
    F = dot(f, Hf)
    if len(n-1)>0:
       grad = 2*f[:-1]*(Hf[:-1]-F)
    else grad empty
    return lse, grad
```

Let's design performant coordinate sweep:
```
def coord_opt(f, stop_time, max_sweeps, seed, min_improve=1e-14):
    n=len(f); L=2*n-1
    f=np.asarray(f,float).copy(); f=np.clip(f,0,None)
    S=f.sum(); if S<... reset
    f/=S
    c=convolve(f); M=max(c)
    best_f=f.copy(); best_M=M
    N=np.empty(L); buf=np.empty(L)
    winner=False
    for sweep...:
       improved=False
       order = list(range(n)); rng.shuffle(order)
       for i in order:
          v=f[i]; R=1-v
          if R < 1e-12: continue
          invR=1/R; invR2=invR*invR
          start=i; end=i+n; diag=2*i
          np.copyto(N, c)
          N[start:end] -= 2.0*v*f
          N[diag] += v*v  # because overshoot
          # if N has negative tiny clip: N[N<0]=0
          if (N < 0).any(): np.maximum(N,0,out=N) # This check costs; maybe just np.maximum(N,0,out=N)
          two_f = 2*f  # can maintain global two_f
          def hval(y):
             if y <= 0: alpha=invR2; beta=0
             elif y >=1: alpha=0; beta=invR
             else: one=1-y; alpha=one*one*invR2; beta=y*invR
             np.copyto(buf, N)
             buf *= alpha
             buf[start:end] += beta*two_f
             # diag adjustment: beta*2v subtract, y^2 add
             buf[diag] += y*y - 2.0*beta*v
             return float(buf.max())
          # golden
          ...
          if hopt < M - tol:
             alpha... beta...
             np.copyto(c, N); c *= alpha; c[start:end] += beta*two_f; c[diag] += y*y - 2.0*beta*v
             np.maximum(c,0,out=c)
             # update f
             f *= (1.0 - y)/R
             f[i] = y
             # f.sum may drift; normalize? The formula sums to 1 if R=1-v exact. fp drift. Maybe f/=f.sum() and recompute c to be safe after each update? If we use c update formula, sum drift small. We can normalize f and scale c? If f scaled by δ, c scales δ^2. We can do: S_new=f.sum(); if abs(S_new-1)>1e-10: f/=S_new; c*= (1/S_new**2). But c formula based on exact R; drift tiny.
             M = float(c.max())
             improved=True
       if not improved: break
       # periodically recompute c to avoid drift every sweep?
       if sweep%5==0:
          f/=f.sum(); c=conv(f); M=max(c)
          if M<best_M: best_M=M; best_f=f.copy()
    return best_f, 2*n*best_M

def _conv(a):
   n=len(a)
   if n <= 800:
      c=np.convolve(a,a,mode='full')
   else:
      L=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
      F=rfft(a,L); c=irfft(F*F,L)[:2*n-1]
   np.maximum(c,0,out=c)
   return c

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic(); budget=max(10,float(budget_s)); hard=t0+budget-20
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent = get_parent()
    # fallback score
    best = normalize(parent) if valid else np.ones(384)/384
    best = np.clip(best,0,1000); best_sc=evaluate_sequence(best)
    def update(seq, sc=None): ...
    # pipeline for a length/init

def _consider(seq, sc=None):
   nonlocal best_seq, best_score
   seq = np.asarray(seq,float).ravel()
   seq = np.where(np.isfinite(seq), seq,0)
   seq = np.clip(seq,0,1000)
   if seq.size==0 or seq.sum()<0.01: return
   if sc is None or not finite: sc=_score(seq)
   exact = evaluate_sequence(seq.tolist())  # exact, but expensive O(n^2) if using np.convolve; n=384/512, okay if not too often. For final and periodic.
   if exact < best_score: update
```

def lbfgs_polish(f0, stop_time, seed, max_phases=6):
   f=normalize(f0)
   c=_conv(f); M=max(c); score=2n*M
   best_f=f.copy(); best_score=score
   rels = [0.8,0.4,0.15,0.05,0.015,0.005]
   # maybe dynamic based on M
   best_u = None
   for phase in range(max_phases):
      if time>stop_time-10: break
      if best_u is None:
         fpos=np.clip(f,1e-12,None); fpos/=f.sum()
         u=np.log(fpos[:-1]/fpos[-1])
      else: u=best_u
      tau=rels[phase % len(rels)] * max(M,1e-12)
      # maybe if tau too small <1e-8? M ~0.002 -> tau 1e-5 min
      tau=max(tau, 1e-6*max(1,n?)?)
      opt = minimize(objfun, u, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':40, 'maxls':20, 'ftol':1e-16, 'gtol':1e-12, 'disp':False})
      if success and finite:
         fnew=anchor(opt.x)
         # coordinate quick? maybe just consider
         cnew=_conv(fnew); Mnew=max(cnew); score=2n*Mnew
         if score < best_score - 1e-12:
             best_f=fnew; best_score=score; M=Mnew; best_u=opt.x
         else:
             # maybe still update u? not if worse
             pass
      # small coordinate after if time
      if time<stop_time-30:
          f2, sc2 = coord_opt(best_f, stop_time-30, max_sweeps=5, seed+phase)
          if sc2<best_score: best...
   return best_f,best_score
```

```
def _coord_opt(f0, stop_time, max_sweeps=500, seed=0):
    f = np.asarray(f0,float).copy()
    f = np.where(np.isfinite(f), f, 0.0)
    f = np.clip(f,0.0,None)
    S=f.sum()
    if S < 1e-10:
        f=np.ones(f.size)/f.size if f.size else np.ones(32)
    else: f/=S
    n=f.size; L=2*n-1
    c=_conv(f)
    M=float(c.max())
    best_f=f.copy(); best_M=M
    if n<2: return best_f, 2*n*best_M
    buf=np.empty(L); N=np.empty(L)
    phi=0.6180339887498949
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    no_improve=0
    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
       if time.monotonic() >= stop_time - 2: break
       improved=False
       order=np.arange(n)
       rng.shuffle(order)
       for i in order:
          if time.monotonic() >= stop_time - 2: break
          v=float(f[i]); R=1.0-v
          if R <= 1e-10:
             # add small mass
             f = (1-1e-8)*f + 1e-8/n
             S=f.sum(); f/=S; c=_conv(f); M=c.max(); continue
          invR=1.0/R; invR2=invR*invR
          start=i; end=i+n; diag=2*i
          np.copyto(N, c)
          N[start:end] -= 2.0*v*f
          N[diag] += v*v
          # clip negative tiny
          # np.maximum(N, 0.0, out=N)  # might be expensive but okay
          # To save check, do if N.min()<0? N.min costs. Just np.maximum.
          np.maximum(N, 0.0, out=N)
          two_f = 2.0*f
          # define hval without nested function overhead? Could define inner; nested call 32*384 times, overhead maybe significant. We can inline golden loop with a local function for readability. Python function call 35*384=13k, fine overhead ~0.01s.
          def hval(y, N=N, f=f, start=start, end=end, diag=diag, invR2=invR2, invR=invR, v=v, buf=buf, two_f=two_f, L=L):
              yy=float(y)
              one=1.0-yy
              alpha=one*one*invR2
              beta=yy*invR
              np.copyto(buf, N)
              buf *= alpha
              buf[start:end] += beta * two_f
              buf[diag] += yy*yy - beta * two_v? two_v =2v
              return float(buf.max())
```

Implement `_lbfgs_polish`:
```
def _lbfgs_polish(f0, stop_time, seed=0, phases=5):
    f = _normalize(f0)
    n=f.size
    if n<2 or time>=stop_time-10: return f, _score_norm(f)
    c=_conv(f); M=max(c); score=2n*M
    best_f=f.copy(); best_score=score
    best_u=None
    rels=[0.2,0.08,0.03,0.01,0.004,0.0015]
    attempts=0
    while time < stop_time - 20 and attempts < phases*2:
        ph = attempts % len(rels)
        # If score near local and tau very small, maybe break after no improve
        tau = max(rels[ph]*M, 1e-9)
        if best_u is None:
           u = _u_from_f(best_f)
        else:
           # small random perturbation in u low freq?
           u = best_u + 0.05*np.random.default_rng(seed+attempts).normal(size=n-1)
        try:
           res = minimize(_make_obj(u,tau,n), u, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B',
                          options={'maxiter':35, 'maxls':20, 'ftol':1e-15, 'gtol':1e-12, 'disp':False})
        except Exception:
           res=None
        if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x') and res.x is not None:
           fnew = _f_from_u(res.x)
           cnew=_conv(fnew); Mnew=max(cnew); scorenew=2n*Mnew
           if scorenew < best_score - 1e-12:
               best_f=fnew; best_score=scorenew; M=Mnew; best_u=res.x
               attempts=0? # reset phases to redo smaller tau? maybe keep
           else:
               attempts += 1
               if scorenew > best_score*(1+0.05): # avoid bad u
                   best_u=None? no
        # If time left < 25 break
    return _normalize(best_f), best_score
```
But if best improves, we should restart phase sequence from larger tau? Maybe continue with smaller tau from better f. Use `phase_idx = attempts % len(rels)`. If improve, maybe `attempts=0` to use larger tau again? But from improved f, smaller tau maybe better. We can just progress. Max attempts 6.

The objective function closure must be defined for each tau,n and perhaps current f? It doesn't depend on current f except tau,n. We can define:
```
def _make_obj(tau,n):
   local={}
   def obj(u):
      f=_f_from_u(u)
      c=_conv(f)
      z=c/tau
      m=z.max()
      J=tau*(m+math.log(np.exp(z-m).sum()))
      if not finite: return 1e100, np.zeros(n-1)
      p=np.exp(z-m); p/=p.sum()
      Hf=np.correlate(p,f,mode='valid')
      F=float(np.dot(f,Hf))
      grad=2.0*f[:-1]*(Hf[:-1]-F)
      if not finite: return J, zeros
      return J, grad
   return obj
```
No need derivative of f? We derived. Double-check derivative for anchor with objective J = tau*lse(c/tau). dJ/du_j = f_j(1-f_j)∂J/∂f_j - f_j∑ f_i ∂J/∂f_i? def _lp_blend(f, stop_time, time_limit=8):
```
   n=len(f); c=_conv(f); M=max(c)
   A = np.zeros((2*n-1,n)); for j in range(n): A[j:j+n,j]=f
   res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(M), bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'time_limit':time_limit,'presolve':True})
   if ok:
      g=res.x/g.sum(); for t in [0.2,0.4,0.6,1.0?]: h=(1-t)f+t*g; h=normalize; score; accept best
```

`_clean_seq`:
```
def _clean_seq(a):
    if a is None: return np.array([])
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    s=arr.sum()
    if s < 0.01: return np.ones(384)/384? 
    return arr
```
For optimization normalize separately.

`_normalize(f)`:
```
def _normalize(f):
    f=np.asarray(f,dtype=float).ravel()
    f=np.where(np.isfinite(f),f,0.0); f=np.clip(f,0.0,None)
    s=f.sum()
    if s < 1e-12:
       n=f.size if f.size>0 else 384
       return np.ones(n)/n
    return f/s
```
For exact return, we might return normalized sum1, not clip 1000 (sum1 ensures ≤1). But if f size 0, fallback.

`_conv`:
```
def _conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([0.0])
    if n<=800:
       c=np.convolve(a,a,mode='full')
    else:
       L=2*n-1
       m=next_fast_len(L)
       F=rfft(a,m)
       c=irfft(F*F,m)[:L]
    np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
    return c
```
For n=1, conv [a²].

`_score_norm(f)` assumes normalized but can normalize:
```
def _score_norm(f):
    f=_normalize(f)
    n=f.size
    c=_conv(f); m=float(c.max()); return 2.0*n*m
```

`_exact_score(seq)`:
```
def _exact_score(seq):
    try:
       ev = globals().get('evaluate_sequence')
       if ev is None: ...
       val=ev([float(x) for x in seq])
    except Exception: val=math.inf
    if not math.isfinite(val): val=math.inf
    return val
```
Local fallback if ev missing: use `_score_norm`. But harness preimports.

Now, `_coord_opt` code. Let's write carefully.

```
def _coord_opt(f0, stop_time, max_sweeps=500, seed=0):
    f = _normalize(f0)
    n = f.size
    if n == 0:
        f = np.ones(32); n=32
    if n == 1:
        return f, 2.0
    L=2*n-1
    c = _conv(f)
    M = float(c.max())
    best_f = f.copy()
    best_M = M
    if M < 1e-300: return f, 0.0
    g1 = 80 if n <= 600 else 48
    g2 = 32 if n <= 600 else 20
    g3 = 16 if n <= 600 else 12
    Gmax = g1
    vals = np.empty((Gmax, L))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    no_improve=0
    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
        if time.monotonic() >= stop_time - 2.0: break
        improved=False
        order=np.arange(n); rng.shuffle(order)
        for i in order:
            if time.monotonic() >= stop_time - 2.0: break
            v=float(f[i]); R=1.0-v
            if R <= 1e-10:
                # redistribute a little
                f = (1.0-1e-8)*f + 1e-8/n
                f = _normalize(f); c=_conv(f); M=float(c.max())
                if M < best_M - 1e-14:
                    best_M=M; best_f=f.copy()
                continue
            invR=1.0/R; invR2=invR*invR
            start=i; end=i+n; diag=2*i
            np.copyto(N, c) # Need N allocated before
```
Need allocate `N` length L before loops. `np.empty(L)`.

But inside coordinate, `N` reused. `two_f = 2.0*f` (could allocate each). Maybe maintain `two_f = 2.0*f` and update after f changes. But since f may change within sweep, at each coordinate we need current. We can do `two_f = 2.0 * f` length n. Accept may update f, so next coordinate gets new. Cost n per coord, small. Or `np.multiply(f, 2.0, out=two_f)` with prealloc two_f length n and update after accepts. Let's prealloc `two_f = np.empty(n)` and fill `two_f[:] = 2*f`; after accept `two_f[:] = 2*f`.

```
N = c.copy(); N[start:end] -= 2.0*v*f; N[diag] += v*v; np.maximum(N,0,out=N)
two_v = 2.0*v
yhi = 1.0 - 1e-12
if R < 1e-4:
    yhi = max(0.0, 1.0 - 0.5*R)
```
Make sure yhi>=0. If yhi<1e-8? skip.

Define eval_grid inside loop:
```
def eval_grid(y):
    m=y.size
    if m > Gmax: y = y[:Gmax] # not
    one = 1.0 - y
    alpha = (one * one) * invR2
    gamma = y * one * invR
    vbuf = vals[:m, :]
    # Multiply alpha[:,None] * N into vbuf
    np.multiply(alpha[:, None], N, out=vbuf)
    # Add gamma*E
    # To avoid large temp, use loop? Use direct.
    vbuf[:, start:end] += gamma[:, None] * two_f
    vbuf[:, diag] += y * y - gamma * two_v
    return vbuf.max(axis=1)
```
But `vals` is shared; when vbuf[:, start:end] += ..., if slice overlaps diag, diag adjustment after. Good. However, `vals[:m,:]` may contain previous data beyond m? We overwrite all entries via multiply. Good.

Potential issue: `np.multiply(alpha[:, None], N, out=vbuf)` when N shape (L,) and out shape (m,L), broadcasting works but alpha[:,None] and N not same shape; yes ufunc broadcasts. It may check out shape okay.

Then grid:
```
grid = np.linspace(0.0, yhi, g1)
h = eval_grid(grid)
if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)): ... fallback?
idx = int(np.argmin(h)); ybest=grid[idx]; hbest=h[idx]
# refine
lo = grid[max(0, idx-1)]; hi = grid[min(g1-1, idx+1)]
if hi - lo < yhi*1e-3 or idx==0 or idx==g1-1:
   # maybe expand around ybest a bit
```

Now, L-BFGS code.

```
def _u_from_f(f, eps=1e-12):
    f=np.asarray(f,float).ravel()
    f=np.clip(f, eps, None)
    f/=f.sum()
    if f.size <=1: return np.array([])
    u=np.log(f[:-1]/f[-1])
    return np.clip(u, -100.0, 100.0)

def _f_from_u(u, n):
    u=np.asarray(u,float).ravel()
    if n <=1: return np.ones(1)
    u = np.clip(u, -100, 100)
    m = float(u.max()) if u.size else 0.0
    e = np.exp(u - m)
    denom = float(e.sum()) + 1.0
    f = np.empty(n)
    f[:-1] = e/denom
    f[-1] = 1.0/denom
    return f
```
If u has nan, return uniform? Check.
```
if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)): return np.ones(n)/n
```

Objective:
```
def _make_smooth_objective(tau, n):
    def fun(u):
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
          return 1e100, np.zeros(n-1)
       f = _f_from_u(u,n)
       c = _conv(f)
       if c.size==0: return 1e100, zeros
       z = c / tau
       zmax = z.max()
       expz = np.exp(z - zmax)
       s = expz.sum()
       if s <= 0 or not finite:
          return 1e100, zeros
       J = tau * (zmax + math.log(s))
       p = expz / s
       Hf = np.correlate(p, f, mode='valid')
       F = float(np.dot(f, Hf))
       grad = 2.0 * f[:-1] * (Hf[:-1] - F)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
          grad = np.zeros(n-1)
       return float(J), grad
    return fun
```
If n-1=0, fun returns J, empty grad. minimize with zero variables? skip.

`_lbfgs_polish`:
```
def _lbfgs_polish(f0, stop_time, seed=0, max_phases=6):
    f = _normalize(f0); n=f.size
    if n < 2 or time.monotonic() >= stop_time - 10: return f, _score_norm(f)
    c=_conv(f); M=float(c.max()); score=2n*M
    best_f=f.copy(); best_score=score
    best_u=None
    rels=[0.15,0.06,0.02,0.008,0.003,0.001]
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    phase=0
    stagnant=0
    while time.monotonic() < stop_time - 15 and phase < max_phases*2:
       tau = max(rels[phase % len(rels)] * max(M,1e-12), 1e-9)
       if best_u is None:
          u = _u_from_f(best_f)
       else:
           # small perturb to escape, low amplitude
           u = best_u + 0.05*rng.normal(size=best_u.size)
       ub = np.clip(u, -100,100)
       try:
          res = minimize(_make_smooth_objective(tau,n), ub, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B',
                         options={'maxiter':40, 'maxls':20, 'ftol':1e-15, 'gtol':1e-12, 'disp':False})
       except Exception:
          res=None
       improved=False
       if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x') and res.x is not None:
          fnew = _f_from_u(np.asarray(res.x,float), n)
          cnew = _conv(fnew); Mnew = float(cnew.max()); scorenew = 2n*Mnew
          if scorenew < best_score - 1e-12:
              best_f=fnew; best_score=scorenew; M=Mnew; best_u=np.asarray(res.x,float); stagnant=0; improved=True
       if not improved:
          stagnant +=1
          if stagnant >= len(rels): break
       phase +=1
    # final local score exact? use c from best_f maybe
    c=_conv(best_f); M=float(c.max()); score=2n*M
    return best_f, score
```
If L-BFGS objective uses tau based on M best; after phases, if M not improved, tau gets smaller but stagnant break. max_phases 6.

The objective closure created each phase; could be costly but okay. `minimize` may call objective many times; each `_conv` FFT/direct. For n=384, okay.

Potential: `minimize` with `jac=True` expects fun returns (f,g). It passes u ndarray. Good.

Could `minimize` use `maxiter` 40 and `gtol` 1e-12; gradient may be ~1e-5, so it will run. If gradient ~1e-13, stop. Good.

```
def _lp_escape(f0, stop_time, seed=0, max_solves=2, solve_time=6.0):
    if linprog is None: return f0,_score_norm(f0)
    f=_normalize(f0); n=f.size
    if n > 512: return ... # avoid big
    c=_conv(f); M=max
    best_f=f.copy(); best_score=2n*M
    rng=...
    for k in range(max_solves):
       if time >= stop_time - 30: break
       c=_conv(f); M=max(c)
       if M <= 0: break
       # check time
       A = np.zeros((2*n-1, n))
       for j in range(n): A[j:j+n, j] = f
       tlimit = max(1.0, min(solve_time, stop_time - time - 10))
       try:
          res = linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2*n-1, M), bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'presolve':True,'time_limit':tlimit})
       except: break
       if res.status not in [0,2]? HiGHS status 0 optimal, 3 time limit maybe res.x? If status==3 with x? Use if res.x is not None.
       if res.x is not None:
           g=np.clip(res.x,0,None); gs=g.sum()
           if gs>1e-12:
              g/=gs
              # line search
              best_blend=f; best_sc=best_score
              for t in (0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7,1.0):
                  h=(1-t)*f + t*g; h=_normalize(h)
                  sc=_score_norm(h)
                  if sc < best_sc - 1e-13: best_blend=h; best_sc=sc
              if best_sc < best_score - 1e-13:
                  f=best_blend; best_f=f; best_score=best_score? update
       # short coord
       if time < stop_time - 15:
          f2, sc2 = _coord_opt(f, min(stop_time-5, time+8), 20, seed+k)
          if sc2 < best_score - 1e-13: f=f2; best_f=f2; best_score=sc2
       if f is best? 
    return best_f, _score_norm(best_f)
```

Now, `_resample` and `_patterns`.

```
def _resample(f, n):
   f=_normalize(f)
   if len(f)==n: return f.copy()
   old = np.linspace(0,1,len(f)); new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   return np.interp(new, old, f)
```
Patterns:
```
def _make_patterns(n, base):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   l = [base.copy() if len(base)==n else _resample(base,n)]
   l.append(np.ones(n))
   l.append(0.25 + 0.75*np.cos(np.pi*x)**2)
   l.append(0.5 + 0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*x))
   l.append(0.5 + 0.5*np.cos(4*np.pi*x))
   l.append(0.2 + 0.8*np.sin(np.pi*x)**2)
   l.append(0.3 + 0.7*np.exp(-((x-0.5)**2)/0.05))
   # maybe lambe? 
```

Now, `_perturb`:
```
def _perturb(f, amp, rng):
   n=len(f); x=np.arange(n)
   noise = amp*rng.normal(size=n)
   for k in (1,2,3):
      noise += (amp/ (k+1)) * np.sin(2*np.pi*k*x/n + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
   g = f + noise
   g=np.clip(g,0,None); s=g.sum()
   if s < 0.01: return f.copy()
   return g/s
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic(); budget=max(10.0,float(budget_s)); hard=t0+budget-15
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent=_get_parent()
    # initial best
    best_seq=_normalize(parent)
    if best_seq.size==0 or best_seq.sum()<0.01:
       best_seq=np.ones(384)/384
    try: best_score=_exact_score(best_seq)
    except: best_score=math.inf
    if not finite: best_score=_score_norm(best_seq); best_seq=_normalize(best_seq)
    def _consider(seq):
       nonlocal best_seq, best_score
       seq=_normalize(seq)
       if seq.size==0: return
       sc=_exact_score(seq)
       if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
           best_seq=seq.copy(); best_score=sc
    _consider(best_seq)
    # if budget too small, validate and return
    if hard - t0 < 30:
       return [float(x) for x in best_seq]
```

Pseudo final:
```
def _coord_opt(f0, stop_time, max_sweeps=500, seed=0):
    f=_normalize(f0)
    n=f.size
    if n<1: f=np.ones(32); n=32
    if n==1: return f, 2.0
    L=2*n-1
    c=_conv(f)
    M=float(c.max())
    best_f=f.copy(); best_M=M
    if M <= 1e-300: return best_f, 0.0
    g1 = 80 if n <= 700 else 48
    g2 = 32 if n <= 700 else 20
    g3 = 16 if n <= 700 else 12
    N=np.empty(L)
    two_f=np.empty(n)
    two_f +=? 
    vals=np.empty((g1, L))
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    no_improve=0
    def refresh_two(): two_f[:] = 2.0*f
    refresh_two()
    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
       if time.monotonic() >= stop_time - 2: break
       improved=False
       order=np.arange(n); rng.shuffle(order)
       for i in order:
          if time.monotonic() >= stop_time - 2: break
          v=float(f[i]); R=1.0-v
          if R <= 1e-10:
              f = (1.0-1e-8)*f + 1e-8/n
              f = _normalize(f); c=_conv(f); M=float(c.max()); refresh_two()
              if M < best_M - 1e-14: best_M=M; best_f=f.copy()
              continue
          invR=1.0/R; invR2=invR*invR
          start=i; end=i+n; diag=2*i
          np.copyto(N, c)
          N[start:end] -= 2.0*v*f
          N[diag] += v*v
          np.maximum(N, 0.0, out=N)
          two_v = 2.0*v
          yhi = 1.0 - 1e-12
          if R < 1e-4:
              yhi = max(0.0, 1.0 - 0.5*R)
          if yhi <= 0:
              continue
          def eval_grid(y):
              m=int(y.size)
              if m > g1:
                  y = np.linspace(y[0], y[-1], g1)
                  m=g1
              one=1.0-y
              alpha=(one*one)*invR2
              gamma=y*one*invR
              vb=vals[:m,:]
              np.multiply(alpha[:,None], N, out=vb)
              # add E
              vb[:, start:end] += gamma[:,None] * two_f
              vb[:, diag] += y*y - gamma*two_v
              return vb.max(axis=1)
          grid=np.linspace(0.0, yhi, g1)
          h=eval_grid(grid)
          if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
              continue
          idx=int(np.argmin(h))
          ybest=float(grid[idx]); hbest=float(h[idx])
          # refine
          if idx > 0 and idx < g1-1:
              lo=float(grid[idx-1]); hi=float(grid[idx+1])
          elif idx == 0:
              lo=0.0; hi=float(grid[1]) if g1>1 else yhi
          else:
              lo=float(grid[-2]); hi=yhi
          if hi <= lo + 1e-15:
              hi = min(yhi, lo + max(1e-6, yhi*1e-3))
          grid2=np.linspace(lo, hi, g2)
          h2=eval_grid(grid2)
          if np.all(np.isfinite(h2)):
              idx2=int(np.argmin(h2)); ybest=float(grid2[idx2]); hbest=float(h2[idx2]); lo2=float(grid2[0]); hi2=float(grid2[-1])
          else: continue
          span=max(hi-lo, 1e-6)
          lo3=max(0.0, ybest - 0.4*span)
          hi3=min(yhi, ybest + 0.4*span)
          if hi3 > lo3 + 1e-15:
              grid3=np.linspace(lo3, hi3, g3)
              h3=eval_grid(grid3)
              if np.all(np.isfinite(h3)):
                  idx3=int(np.argmin(h3)); ybest=float(grid3[idx3]); hbest=float(h3[idx3])
          if hbest < M - 1e-13:
              y=float(ybest)
              old_f=f.copy(); old_c=c.copy(); old_M=M
              one=max(0.0, 1.0-y)
              f *= one / R
              f[i]=y
              S=float(f.sum())
              if S <= 1e-12:
                  f=old_f; c=old_c; M=old_M; refresh_two(); continue
              f /= S
              c=_conv(f)
              Mnew=float(c.max())
              if Mnew < old_M - 1e-14:
                  M=Mnew; improved=True; refresh_two()
                  if M < best_M - 1e-14:
                      best_M=M; best_f=f.copy()
              else:
                  f=old_f; c=old_c; M=old_M; refresh_two()
       if improved:
          no_improve=0
       else:
          no_improve += 1
          if no_improve >= 2:
             break
    f=_normalize(f); c=_conv(f); M=float(c.max())
    if M < best_M - 1e-14:
       best_M=M; best_f=f.copy()
    return best_f.copy(), 2.0*best_f.size*best_M
```

Define `_normalize`:
```
def _normalize(f):
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if f.size == 0:
        f = np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64)
    f = np.where(np.isfinite(f), f, 0.0)
    f = np.clip(f, 0.0, None)
    s = float(f.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(f.size, dtype=np.float64) / f.size
    return f / s
```
This always sum1.

```
def _lp_escape(f0, stop_time, seed=0, max_solves=2, solve_time=6.0):
    if linprog is None: return _normalize(f0), _score_norm(f0)
    f=_normalize(f0); n=f.size
    if n > 600: return f, _score_norm(f)
    c=_conv(f); best_score=2*n*c.max(); best_f=f.copy()
    for k in range(max_solves):
       if time.monotonic() >= stop_time - 25: break
       c=_conv(f); M=float(c.max())
       if M <= 0: break
       tlim = max(1.0, min(float(solve_time), stop_time - time.monotonic() - 15))
       if tlim <= 0.5: break
       A = np.zeros((2*n-1, n))
       for j in range(n): A[j:j+n,j]=f
       try:
          res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2*n-1, M), bounds=[(0.0,100.0)]*n, method='highs', options={'presolve':True,'time_limit':tlim})
       except Exception: break
       if res is None or res.x is None: break
       g=np.asarray(res.x,float).clip(0,None)
       gs=float(g.sum())
       if gs > 1e-12:
           g/=gs
           cur_f=f.copy(); cur_sc=best_score
           for t in (0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7,1.0):
               h=(1-t)*cur_f + t*g
               h=_normalize(h)
               sc=_score_norm(h)
               if sc < cur_sc - 1e-13:
                   cur_f=h; cur_sc=sc
           if cur_sc < best_score - 1e-13:
               f=cur_f; best_f=f.copy(); best_score=cur_sc
       if time.monotonic() < stop_time - 20:
           f2, sc2 = _coord_opt(f, min(stop_time - 10, time.monotonic() + 8.0), 20, seed+100+k)
           if sc2 < best_score - 1e-13:
               f=f2; best_f=f2.copy(); best_score=sc2
    return best_f, _score_norm(best_f)
```
Note: `res.x` may be None if status infeasible; break. If res.x present but infeasible? HiGHS maybe. Check.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
from scipy.optimize import minimize
try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None


def _get_parent():
    h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if h is None:
        return np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64)
    try:
        p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        p = np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size == 0:
        p = np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if p.sum() <= 1e-15:
        p = np.ones(p.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return p


def _normalize(f):
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if f.size == 0:
        f = np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64)
    f = np.where(np.isfinite(f), f, 0.0)
    f = np.clip(f, 0.0, None)
    s = float(f.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(f.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(f.size)
    return f / s


def _conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 800:
        c = np.convolve(a, a, mode="full")
    else:
        L = 2 * n - 1
        m = next_fast_len(L)
        F = rfft(a, m)
        c = irfft(F * F, m)[:L]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def _score_norm(f):
    f = _normalize(f)
    n = f.size
    if n == 0:
        return math.inf
    c = _conv(f)
    m = float(c.max())
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return math.inf
    return 2.0 * float(n) * m


def _exact_score(seq):
    try:
        ev = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)
        if ev is None:
            return _score_norm(seq)
        val = ev([float(x) for x in np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()])
        val = float(val)
        return val if math.isfinite(val) else math.inf
    except Exception:
        return math.inf


def _coord_opt(f0, stop_time, max_sweeps=500, seed=0):
    f = _normalize(f0)
    n = f.size
    if n < 1:
        f = np.ones(32, dtype=np.float64)
        n = 32
    if n == 1:
        return f.copy(), 2.0

    L = 2 * n - 1
    c = _conv(f)
    M = float(c.max())
    best_f = f.copy()
    best_M = M
    if M <= 1e-300:
        return best_f, 0.0

    g1 = 80 if n <= 700 else 48
    g2 = 32 if n <= 700 else 20
    g3 = 16 if n <= 700 else 12

    N = np.empty(L, dtype=np.float64)
    two_f = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    two_f[:] = 2.0 * f
    vals = np.empty((g1, L), dtype=np.float64)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    no_improve = 0

    def refresh_two():
        two_f[:] = 2.0 * f

    for _sweep in range(max_sweeps):
        if time.monotonic() >= stop_time - 2.0:
            break
        improved = False
        order = np.arange(n)
        rng.shuffle(order)

        for i in order:
            if time.monotonic() >= stop_time - 2.0:
                break

            v = float(f[i])
            R = 1.0 - v
            if R <= 1e-10:
                old_M = M
                f = (1.0 - 1e-8) * f + (1e-8 / float(n))
                f = _normalize(f)
                c = _conv(f)
                M = float(c.max())
                refresh_two()
                if M < best_M - 1e-14:
                    best_M = M
                    best_f = f.copy()
                if M < old_M - 1e-14:
                    improved = True
                continue

            invR = 1.0 / R
            invR2 = invR * invR
            start = i
            end = i + n
            diag = 2 * i

            np.copyto(N, c)
            N[start:end] -= 2.0 * v * f
            N[diag] += v * v
            np.maximum(N, 0.0, out=N)
            two_v = 2.0 * v

            yhi = 1.0 - 1e-12
            if R < 1e-4:
                yhi = max(0.0, 1.0 - 0.5 * R)
            if yhi <= 0.0:
                continue

            def eval_grid(y):
                y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
                m = int(y.size)
                if m == 0:
                    return np.array([])
                if m > g1:
                    y = np.linspace(y[0], y[-1], g1)
                    m = g1
                one = 1.0 - y
                alpha = (one * one) * invR2
                gamma = y * one * invR
                vb = vals[:m, :]
                np.multiply(alpha[:, None], N, out=vb)
                vb[:, start:end] += gamma[:, None] * two_f
                vb[:, diag] += y * y - gamma * two_v
                return vb.max(axis=1)

            grid = np.linspace(0.0, yhi, g1)
            h = eval_grid(grid)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
                continue
            idx = int(np.argmin(h))
            ybest = float(grid[idx])
            hbest = float(h[idx])

            if idx > 0 and idx < g1 - 1:
                lo = float(grid[idx - 1])
                hi = float(grid[idx + 1])
            elif idx == 0:
                lo = 0.0
                hi = float(grid[1]) if g1 > 1 else yhi
            else:
                lo = float(grid[-2])
                hi = yhi

            if hi <= lo + 1e-15:
                hi = min(yhi, lo + max(1e-6, yhi * 1e-3))

            grid2 = np.linspace(lo, hi, g2)
            h2 = eval_grid(grid2)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(h2)):
                continue
            idx2 = int(np.argmin(h2))
            ybest = float(grid2[idx2])
            hbest = float(h2[idx2])
            span = max(float(grid2[-1]) - float(grid2[0]), 1e-6)

            lo3 = max(0.0, ybest - 0.4 * span)
            hi3 = min(yhi, ybest + 0.4 * span)
            if hi3 > lo3 + 1e-15:
                grid3 = np.linspace(lo3, hi3, g3)
                h3 = eval_grid(grid3)
                if np.all(np.isfinite(h3)):
                    idx3 = int(np.argmin(h3))
                    ybest = float(grid3[idx3])
                    hbest = float(h3[idx3])

            if hbest < M - 1e-13:
                y = float(ybest)
                old_f = f.copy()
                old_c = c.copy()
                old_M = M

                one = max(0.0, 1.0 - y)
                f *= one / R
                f[i] = y
                S = float(f.sum())
                if S <= 1e-12:
                    f = old_f
                    c = old_c
                    M = old_M
                    refresh_two()
                    continue
                f /= S
                c = _conv(f)
                Mnew = float(c.max())

                if Mnew < old_M - 1e-14:
                    M = Mnew
                    improved = True
                    refresh_two()
                    if M < best_M - 1e-14:
                        best_M = M
                        best_f = f.copy()
                else:
                    f = old_f
                    c = old_c
                    M = old_M
                    refresh_two()

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 2:
                break

    f = _normalize(f)
    c = _conv(f)
    M = float(c.max())
    if M < best_M - 1e-14:
        best_M = M
        best_f = f.copy()
    return best_f.copy(), 2.0 * float(best_f.size) * best_M


def _u_from_f(f):
    f = _normalize(f)
    n = f.size
    if n <= 1:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    fp = np.clip(f, 1e-12, None)
    fp /= float(fp.sum())
    u = np.log(fp[:-1] / max(fp[-1], 1e-300))
    return np.clip(u, -100.0, 100.0)


def _f_from_u(u, n):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    u = np.clip(u, -100.0, 100.0)
    if u.size != n - 1:
        u = u[:n - 1] if u.size > n - 1 else np.pad(u, (0, max(0, n - 1 - u.size)))
    m = float(u.max()) if u.size else 0.0
    e = np.exp(u - m)
    denom = float(e.sum()) + 1.0
    if denom <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(denom):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    f = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    f[:-1] = e / denom
    f[-1] = 1.0 / denom
    return f


def _lbfgs_polish(f0, stop_time, seed=0, max_phases=6):
    f = _normalize(f0)
    n = f.size
    if n < 2:
        return f, _score_norm(f)
    if time.monotonic() >= stop_time - 10.0:
        return f, _score_norm(f)

    c = _conv(f)
    M = float(c.max())
    best_f = f.copy()
    best_score = 2.0 * float(n) * M
    best_u = None

    rels = [0.15, 0.06, 0.02, 0.008, 0.003, 0.001]
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    phase = 0
    stagnant = 0

    def make_obj(tau):
        def fun(u):
            try:
                u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
                    return 1e100, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
                fcur = _f_from_u(u, n)
                fcur = _normalize(fcur)
                curc = _conv(fcur)
                z = curc / tau
                zmax = float(z.max())
                ez = np.exp(z - zmax)
                s = float(ez.sum())
                if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
                    return 1e100, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
                J = tau * (zmax + math.log(s))
                p = ez / s
                Hf = np.correlate(p, fcur, mode="valid")
                F = float(np.dot(fcur, Hf))
                grad = 2.0 * fcur[:-1] * (Hf[:-1] - F)
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
                    grad = np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
                return float(J), grad
            except Exception:
                return 1e100, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        return fun

    while time.monotonic() < stop_time - 15.0 and phase < 2 * max_phases:
        tau = max(rels[phase % len(rels)] * max(M, 1e-12), 1e-9)
        if best_u is None:
            u = _u_from_f(best_f)
        else:
            u = np.asarray(best_u, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            u += 0.05 * rng.normal(size=u.size)
            u = np.clip(u, -100.0, 100.0)

        improved = False
        try:
            res = minimize(
                make_obj(tau),
                u,
                jac=True,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={
                    "maxiter": 40,
                    "maxls": 20,
                    "ftol": 1e-15,
                    "gtol": 1e-12,
                    "disp": False,
                },
            )
            if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
                fnew = _f_from_u(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64), n)
                fnew = _normalize(fnew)
                cnew = _conv(fnew)
                Mnew = float(cnew.max())
                scorenew = 2.0 * float(n) * Mnew
                if scorenew < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_f = fnew
                    best_score = scorenew
                    M = Mnew
                    best_u = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                    stagnant = 0
                    improved = True
        except Exception:
            pass

        if not improved:
            stagnant += 1
            if stagnant >= len(rels):
                break
        phase += 1

    c = _conv(best_f)
    M = float(c.max())
    return best_f.copy(), 2.0 * float(best_f.size) * M


def _lp_escape(f0, stop_time, seed=0, max_solves=2, solve_time=6.0):
    if linprog is None:
        f = _normalize(f0)
        return f, _score_norm(f)
    f = _normalize(f0)
    n = f.size
    if n > 600 or n < 2:
        return f, _score_norm(f)

    c = _conv(f)
    M = float(c.max())
    best_f = f.copy()
    best_score = 2.0 * float(n) * M

    for k in range(max_solves):
        if time.monotonic() >= stop_time - 25.0:
            break
        c = _conv(f)
        M = float(c.max())
        if M <= 0.0:
            break

        tlim = max(1.0, min(float(solve_time), stop_time - time.monotonic() - 15.0))
        if tlim <= 0.5:
            break

        try:
            A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
            for j in range(n):
                A[j:j + n, j] = f
            res = linprog(
                -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64),
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=np.full(2 * n - 1, M, dtype=np.float64),
                bounds=[(0.0, 100.0)] * n,
                method="highs",
                options={"presolve": True, "time_limit": float(tlim)},
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if res is None or getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
            break

        g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        g = np.clip(g, 0.0, None)
        gs = float(g.sum())
        if gs > 1e-12:
            g /= gs
            cur_f = f.copy()
            cur_sc = best_score
            for t in (0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 1.0):
                h = (1.0 - t) * f + t * g
                h = _normalize(h)
                sc = _score_norm(h)
                if sc < cur_sc - 1e-13:
                    cur_f = h
                    cur_sc = sc
            if cur_sc < best_score - 1e-13:
                f = cur_f
                best_f = f.copy()
                best_score = cur_sc

        if time.monotonic() < stop_time - 20.0:
            try:
                f2, sc2 = _coord_opt(
                    f,
                    min(stop_time - 10.0, time.monotonic() + 8.0),
                    max_sweeps=20,
                    seed=100 + k,
                )
                if sc2 < best_score - 1e-13:
                    f = f2
                    best_f = f2.copy()
                    best_score = sc2
            except Exception:
                pass

    return best_f, _score_norm(best_f)


def _resample(f, n):
    f = _normalize(f)
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if f.size == n:
        return f.copy()
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, f.size)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return np.interp(new, old, f).astype(np.float64)


def _perturb(f, amp, rng):
    f = _normalize(f)
    n = f.size
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    noise = amp * rng.normal(size=n)
    for k in (1, 2, 3):
        noise += (amp / (k + 1.0)) * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * k * x / float(n) + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
    g = f + noise
    g = np.clip(g, 0.0, None)
    s = float(g.sum())
    if s < 1e-8:
        return f.copy()
    return g / s


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    run_budget = max(10.0, float(budget_s))
    hard = t0 + max(5.0, run_budget - 15.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    parent = _get_parent()
    if parent.size > 1024:
        parent = _resample(parent, 1024)

    best_seq = _normalize(parent)
    try:
        best_score = _exact_score(best_seq)
    except Exception:
        best_score = math.inf
    if not math.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = _score_norm(best_seq)
    if not math.isfinite(best_score):
        best_seq = np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64) / 384.0
        best_score = 2.0

    def consider(seq):
        nonlocal best_seq, best_score
        try:
            seq = _normalize(seq)
            if seq.size == 0 or float(seq.sum()) < 0.01:
                return
            sc = _exact_score(seq)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_seq = seq.copy()
                best_score = sc
        except Exception:
            pass

    consider(best_seq)

    if hard - t0 < 25.0:
        best_seq = _normalize(best_seq)
        return [float(x) for x in best_seq]

    # Main optimization at the parent length.
    f_main = best_seq.copy()
    end1 = min(hard, t0 + max(30.0, run_budget * 0.20))
    f_main, _ = _coord_opt(f_main, end1, max_sweeps=800, seed=11)
    consider(f_main)

    end2 = min(hard, t0 + max(60.0, run_budget * 0.42))
    if time.monotonic() < end2 - 20.0:
        f_main, _ = _lbfgs_polish(best_seq.copy(), end2, seed=22, max_phases=6)
        consider(f_main)

    end3 = min(hard, t0 + max(90.0, run_budget * 0.62))
    f_main, _ = _coord_opt(best_seq.copy(), end3, max_sweeps=800, seed=33)
    consider(f_main)

    # Sparse LP escape, only where the linear-program workspace is manageable.
    end4 = min(hard, t0 + max(120.0, run_budget * 0.72))
    if (
        time.monotonic() < end4 - 40.0
        and linprog is not None
        and best_seq.size <= 512
    ):
        f_main, _ = _lp_escape(best_seq.copy(), end4, seed=44, max_solves=2, solve_time=6.0)
        consider(f_main)

    # Stochastic smooth restarts from the best point.
    end5 = min(hard, t0 + max(150.0, run_budget * 0.82))
    for k in range(4):
        if time.monotonic() > end5 - 45.0:
            break
        amp = 0.03 + 0.025 * k
        f_p = _perturb(best_seq, amp, rng)
        stop_p = min(end5, time.monotonic() + 75.0)
        if time.monotonic() < stop_p - 35.0:
            f_p, _ = _lbfgs_polish(f_p, min(stop_p, time.monotonic() + 30.0), seed=55 + k, max_phases=3)
        f_p, _ = _coord_opt(f_p, stop_p, max_sweeps=80, seed=66 + k)
        consider(f_p)

    # Optional resolution exploration.
    explore_n = None
    if best_seq.size < 512:
        explore_n = 512
    elif best_seq.size < 768 and run_budget > 900.0:
        explore_n = 768

    if explore_n is not None:
        exp_stop = min(hard - 90.0, t0 + max(180.0, run_budget * 0.90))
        if time.monotonic() < exp_stop - 70.0:
            f_exp = _resample(best_seq, explore_n)
            if time.monotonic() < exp_stop - 60.0:
                f_exp, _ = _lbfgs_polish(f_exp, min(exp_stop, time.monotonic() + 55.0),
                                         seed=1000 + explore_n, max_phases=4)
            consider(f_exp)
            f_exp, _ = _coord_opt(f_exp, exp_stop, max_sweeps=400, seed=2000 + explore_n)
            consider(f_exp)

    # Final intensive polish of the exact best candidate.
    end_final = hard - 8.0
    if time.monotonic() < end_final - 5.0:
        f_final = _coord_opt(best_seq.copy(), end_final, max_sweeps=600, seed=9999)
        consider(f_final)

    best_seq = _normalize(best_seq)
    try:
        final_sc = _exact_score(best_seq)
    except Exception:
        final_sc = math.inf

    if not math.isfinite(final_sc):
        best_seq = np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64) / 384.0
        try:
            final_sc = _exact_score(best_seq)
        except Exception:
            final_sc = 2.0
        if not math.isfinite(final_sc):
            return [1.0]

    best_seq = np.where(np.isfinite(best_seq), best_seq, 0.0)
    best_seq = np.clip(best_seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if best_seq.size == 0 or float(best_seq.sum()) < 0.01:
        return [1.0]

    return [float(x) for x in best_seq]
```
